- `user32` paint/DC fidelity pass: the audit found four defects. `ReleaseDC` returned 1 unconditionally — it now reports whether the handle named a live DC (`release_dc` returns `dcs.remove(hdc).is_some()`). `EndPaint` released whatever `hdc` the guest's `PAINTSTRUCT` contained, so a corrupt or forged struct could free an unrelated DC — `Window` now tracks `paint_dc` (set by `BeginPaint` after the `PAINTSTRUCT` write succeeds) and `EndPaint` releases that. `paint_msg` synthesized WM_PAINT even for a hidden window — it now waits for `visible`, matching Windows deferring paint until the window is shown. `MoveWindow` ignored `bRepaint` — it now marks the window dirty so the queue generates a WM_PAINT. To keep the visibility model honest, `CreateWindowEx` now honors `WS_VISIBLE` (0x10000000) at creation instead of always starting hidden. `rustfmt --edition 2024` on the three files, `cargo clippy -p winapi --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `THESEUS_HEADLESS=1 cargo test -p winapi` (125 tests), `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2`, and a 25-second headless smoke (`Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 3.0M`, exit 124, no `missing.txt`) all pass. Next blocker: a different scripted path or another audit category.
- Cross-format `Blt` conversion: `blit_copy` warned `blit between different pixel formats` and returned `DD::OK` without writing whenever source and destination depths differed, silently dropping e.g. an 8bpp->16bpp blit. `blit_copy` now builds a `PixelFmt` (bpp + DDPF_RGB channel masks + a cloned palette) for each surface, keeps the verbatim `write_blit` fast path only when the layouts match (`same_layout`: same depth and, for RGB formats, the same masks; indexed-to-indexed copies always verbatim), and otherwise runs `write_blit_convert` — a per-pixel pass through 8-bit RGBA via `mask_to_u8`/`u8_to_mask` channel scaling, palette decode for indexed sources and nearest-color encode for indexed destinations, with the same clip/stretch/src-key/dst-key semantics as `write_blit`. Non-RGB layouts (z-buffer/FourCC) and paletteless indexed surfaces still warn and return `DD::OK`. `pixel_value` was generalized to a 1-4-byte little-endian read so 24bpp pixels work in color-key and conversion paths. New tests cover 8bpp->565 palette expansion, 565->ARGB8888 channel repositioning, the source color key in the convert path, and the paletteless-indexed no-op. `rustfmt --edition 2024`, `cargo clippy -p winapi --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `THESEUS_HEADLESS=1 cargo test -p winapi` (125 tests), `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2`, and a 25-second headless smoke (`Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 3.0M`, exit 124, no `missing.txt`) all pass. Next blocker: a different scripted path (San Francisco race without the city-list Down presses) or another audit category.
- `GetDC(NULL)` scratch-buffer leak fix: each `GetDC(NULL)` heap-allocated a 640x480x4 (~1.2 MB) pixel buffer plus an internal bitmap handle, and `release_dc`/`DeleteDC` removed only the DC — a guest get/release loop would exhaust the 64 MiB process heap and grow the object table without bound. `DC` now carries `owned: Option<(HBITMAP, u32)>` (the internal bitmap handle and the heap pixels, set only by the `GetDC(NULL)` path — window-backed and guest-supplied bitmaps are never owned), `State::release_dc` takes the guest `Memory` and frees both on release, and `DeleteDC` routes through it. The internal HBITMAP is never guest-visible (no `GetCurrentObject` export), so removing it cannot orphan a live handle; currently-selected pen/brush/font handles are deliberately left alone because `SelectObject` returns them to the guest. Lock ordering checked: kernel32 code never calls `gdi32::lock()` and `kernel32::lock()` is only ever taken as a dropped temporary, so the new gdi32->kernel32 edge cannot deadlock. Regression tests cover the owned-buffer object removal and the window-backed no-free path; the pixel free itself is a no-op on the test's empty default process heap. `rustfmt --edition 2024`, `cargo clippy -p winapi --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `THESEUS_HEADLESS=1 cargo test -p winapi` (127 tests), `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2`, and a 25-second headless smoke (`Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 3.0M`, exit 124, no `missing.txt`) all pass. Next blocker: the same leak class for `CreateCompatibleBitmap`/`CreateBitmap` pixels on `DeleteObject` (needs ownership marking on `Object::Bitmap`), or a different scripted path.
- `DeleteObject` fidelity pass: `CreateCompatibleBitmap` heap-allocates its pixel buffer but `DeleteObject` only removed the handle — the pixels leaked, and worse, deleting a bitmap still selected into a DC silently succeeded. `gdi32::State` now tracks `heap_bitmap_pixels` (only `CreateCompatibleBitmap` marks entries; guest- and window-backed pixels never enter it) and `DeleteObject` frees the matching block on removal. `DeleteObject` also now returns false when the handle is still selected into a live DC's bitmap/pen/brush/font slot, matching Windows — which also guarantees a freed bitmap's pixels are never still referenced by a DC. New regression test covers the selected-object rejection and post-release deletion; the pixel free is a no-op on the test's empty default process heap. `rustfmt --edition 2024`, `cargo clippy -p winapi --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `THESEUS_HEADLESS=1 cargo test -p winapi` (128 tests), `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2`, and a 25-second headless smoke (`Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 3.0M`, exit 124, no `missing.txt`) all pass. Next blocker: a different scripted path (cruise/free-roam or a different-city race) or another audit category.
- `UpdateWindow`/`DefWindowProc` paint-fidelity pass: two menu-script variants (`0x0d,0x0d,0x28,0x28,0x0d` and a six-Down version, both with clicks and a key hold) both still selected `GAMETYPE: crash0`/`Loading level 'london'` and reached `Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 11.6M` — the Down presses cycle menu fields but do not reach a different race type, so a distinct-city path needs specific menu coordinates rather than key counts; all warnings in the run were external-content noise (missing LOD models, absent audio banks, DirectMusic, datParser tokens, room-count mismatch). Code changes: `UpdateWindow` previously only set `dirty` and deferred paint to the next queue pump — it now sends `WM_PAINT` synchronously via `SendMessageW` when the update region is non-empty, matching Windows. `DefWindowProc(WM_PAINT)` cleared `dirty` but never pushed the window's pixels to the host — a wndproc that defers got its region validated without any frame rendered; it now also calls `Window::flush`, and ignores a mismatched `hWnd`. New tests cover the WM_PAINT region validation (including the hwnd check) and `UpdateWindow`'s no-dispatch/dispatch-on-dirty paths. `rustfmt --edition 2024`, `cargo clippy -p winapi --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `THESEUS_HEADLESS=1 cargo test -p winapi` (130 tests), `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2`, and a 25-second headless smoke (`Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 3.1M`, exit 124, no `missing.txt`) all pass. Next blocker: another audit category or a scripted path that needs menu coordinates.
- `WaitMessage` blocking fix: it returned after a single `wait_or_poll` even when no message had arrived — a host event that produces no queue message (dropped input before the window exists, a not-yet-due timer) made it return `true` with an empty queue, and a guest that reads the queue without re-checking would spin. It now loops until `peek` actually reports a message, matching Windows' block-until-message semantics; `wait_or_poll` already handles the no-timers (blocking host wait) and timer-pending (1 ms poll) cases. Message-queue audit found no other defects: timer due/coalesce/reschedule logic, hwnd/thread/range filters, the WM_QUIT flag, and DispatchMessage's TIMERPROC routing are all sound. New test covers the immediate-return path. `rustfmt --edition 2024`, `cargo clippy -p winapi --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `THESEUS_HEADLESS=1 cargo test -p winapi` (131 tests), `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2`, and a 25-second headless smoke (`Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 3.1M`, exit 124, no `missing.txt`) all pass. Next blocker: another audit category or a scripted path that needs menu coordinates.
- `ClientToScreen` coordinate fix + shared-state test flake note: `ClientToScreen` returned `true` without touching `lpPoint` — correct only while the window sits at (0,0). It now adds the window's `(x, y)` origin like `MapWindowPoints` does (the model has no frame or caption), treats a null `hWnd` as the desktop (points already screen space), and returns false for a foreign hwnd or unreadable out-pointer; `POINT` gained `PartialEq`/`Eq` for the assertion. Also removed the `wait_message_returns_once_a_message_is_queued` unit test added in the previous milestone: it raced the shared `user32::State` `RefCell`s (unsafely `Sync` across parallel test threads) and flaked once under the full suite — the global queue cannot be exercised safely from parallel tests, and the `WaitMessage` loop itself is unaffected. A `user32` misc/input/dialog/rect audit found no other defects: `DrawTextA`, `wsprintfA/W`, `GetKeyNameTextA`, `GetKeyboardState`, `EnumDisplaySettingsA`, `ChangeDisplaySettingsA`, `SetTimer`/`KillTimer`, the DirectInput event buffers, and the key/button state tracking are all bounds-checked. New `ClientToScreen` test covers the offset, desktop pass-through, and bad-hwnd/bad-pointer paths. `rustfmt --edition 2024`, `cargo clippy -p winapi --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `THESEUS_HEADLESS=1 cargo test -p winapi` (131 tests, 3 consecutive clean runs), `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2`, and a 25-second headless smoke (`Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 3.1M`, exit 124, no `missing.txt`) all pass. Next blocker: another audit category (kernel32 file/console/time, winmm, dsound/dinput, msvcrt) or a scripted path needing menu coordinates.
- `FileTimeToSystemTime` real conversion: it filled the output with zeros instead of converting. The civil-from-days math in `current_system_time` is now a shared `civil_from_unix(seconds, millis)` helper, and `FileTimeToSystemTime` converts the 100ns-since-1601 FILETIME to a real `SYSTEMTIME` (validated against 2000-01-01 Saturday and FILETIME 0 = 1601-01-01 Monday, plus a bad-pointer path); `SYSTEMTIME` gained `FromBytes` for test reads. The rest of `kernel32::time` is sound: `GetTickCount`/QPC both use the host ms clock with a consistent 1000 Hz frequency, `GetTimeZoneInformation` reports UTC as `TIME_ZONE_ID_UNKNOWN` with a zeroed 172-byte struct, and `FileTimeToLocalFileTime`'s identity matches the UTC model. `rustfmt --edition 2024`, `cargo clippy -p winapi --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `THESEUS_HEADLESS=1 cargo test -p winapi` (132 tests), `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2`, and a 25-second headless smoke (`Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 3.1M`, exit 124, no `missing.txt`) all pass. Next blocker: another audit category (kernel32 file/console/dll/nls, winmm, dsound/dinput, msvcrt) or a scripted path needing menu coordinates.
- `kernel32::file` audit + Find* LastError fix: the file surface is otherwise sound — `resolve_path` clamps `..` at the emulated drive root, ReadFile/WriteFile range-check guest buffers, SetFilePointer rejects a negative `FILE_BEGIN` position and carries the 64-bit result through `lpDistanceToMoveHigh`, wildcard matching is linear-time and case-insensitive, and `MM2.lock` is deliberately reported absent so repeat runs start. The one gap: `FindFirstFileA`/`FindNextFileA` returned failure without a `LastError`, so a guest probing directories saw a stale code instead of `ERROR_FILE_NOT_FOUND`/`ERROR_NO_MORE_FILES`/`ERROR_INVALID_HANDLE` — all three paths now set it via `teb_mut`. New test covers the invalid-handle code. `rustfmt --edition 2024`, `cargo clippy -p winapi --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `THESEUS_HEADLESS=1 cargo test -p winapi` (133 tests), `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2`, and a 25-second headless smoke (`Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 3.1M`, exit 124, no `missing.txt`) all pass. Next blocker: another audit category (kernel32 dll/nls/console, winmm, dsound/dinput, msvcrt) or a scripted path needing menu coordinates.
- `msvcrt` audit + two fixes: `__getmainargs` split argv[0] on the first space, truncating a quoted executable path like `"C:\dir with space\game.exe" args` — it now ends the name at the closing quote (unquoted names still split on space). `RAND_STATE` was a `static mut` touched through `unsafe` — it is now an `AtomicU32`, removing the UB-adjacent access pattern with identical LCG semantics (verified: `srand(1)` produces 41 and reseeding restarts the sequence). The rest of the file is sound: `_controlfp`'s abstract/x87 field permutations have round-trip tests, `_initterm` bounds-checks and wrap-guards its function-pointer walk, `__p__fmode`/`__p__commode` lazily allocate process-lifetime ints, and `__getmainargs` reports the documented nonzero failure on unallocatable argv. `rustfmt --edition 2024`, `cargo clippy -p winapi --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `THESEUS_HEADLESS=1 cargo test -p winapi` (134 tests), `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2`, and a 25-second headless smoke (`Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 3.1M`, exit 124, no `missing.txt`) all pass. Next blocker: remaining audit categories (kernel32 dll/nls/console/env, winmm, dsound/dinput, advapi32) or a scripted path needing menu coordinates; no crash or missing-address blocker is reproducible.
|- `kernel32::dll` null/low pointer hardening: `LoadLibraryA` and `GetModuleHandleA` read the module name with `ctx.memory.read_str` before checking whether the pointer was in the emulated null page, so a guest passing a null or sub-0x1000 `lpLibFileName`/`lpModuleName` would read from the protected low address space. Both now reject pointers below `0x1000` (with `GetModuleHandleA` still returning the image base for a true null pointer). Added `get_module_handle_and_load_library_reject_null_and_low_pointers` regression. `rustfmt --edition 2024 win32/winapi/src/kernel32/dll.rs`, `cargo clippy -p winapi --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `THESEUS_HEADLESS=1 cargo test -p winapi` (135 tests), `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2`, and a 20-second headless smoke (`Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 3.1M`, exit 124, no `missing.txt`) all pass. Next blocker: continue the remaining audit (kernel32 env/console/nls, winmm, dsound/dinput, advapi32) or a new scripted path.
|- `mm2` scaffold clippy fix: `cargo clippy -p mm2 --all-targets -- -D warnings` failed on `out/mm2/src/main.rs` with `clippy::collapsible_if`; the nested `if let Some(dir)` / `if let Err(err)` is now a single `let` chain and the target lints clean. `rustfmt --edition 2024 out/mm2/src/main.rs`, `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2`, and a 20-second headless smoke (`RUST_LOG=warn THESEUS_HEADLESS=1 THESEUS_MISSING_ADDRS=out/mm2/missing.txt timeout 20 ./target/fast/mm2 game`) reached `Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 3.1M` with exit 124 and no `out/mm2/missing.txt`. Next blocker: continue the remaining audit (kernel32 dll/nls/console/env, winmm, dsound/dinput, advapi32) or a new scripted path.
|- `kernel32::env` null/low pointer hardening: `GetEnvironmentVariableA`, `SetEnvironmentVariableA`, and the `lpValue` path of `SetEnvironmentVariableA` read name/value strings with `ctx.memory.read_str` before checking for null or sub-0x1000 pointers, so a malformed guest call could read from the emulated null page. All three entry points now reject pointers below `0x1000` (with the documented null semantics preserved: a null `lpName` or `lpValue` is `ERROR_INVALID_PARAMETER` for names and a delete for values). The `environment_variables_round_trip_and_list` test now covers null and 0x500 name/value pointers. `rustfmt --edition 2024 win32/winapi/src/kernel32/env.rs`, `cargo clippy -p winapi --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `THESEUS_HEADLESS=1 cargo test -p winapi` (135 tests), `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2`, and a 20-second headless smoke (`Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 3.1M`, exit 124, no `missing.txt`) all pass. Next blocker: continue the remaining audit (kernel32 console/nls, winmm, dsound/dinput, advapi32) or a new scripted path.
|- `kernel32::nls` null/low pointer hardening: the NLS `read_string_type_*`, `read_multibyte`, and `output_range_fits` helpers did not reject sub-0x1000 source/output pointers, so `GetStringTypeA/W`, `LCMapStringA/W`, `MultiByteToWideChar`, and `WideCharToMultiByte` could read from or write into the emulated null page. All read helpers now require `addr >= 0x1000`, and `output_range_fits` also rejects output buffers in the null page. Existing tests already exercise the positive paths; the `multibyte_to_wide_char_decodes_cp1252_and_includes_nul` test was extended with null and 0x500 source/output assertions. `rustfmt --edition 2024 win32/winapi/src/kernel32/nls.rs`, `cargo clippy -p winapi --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `THESEUS_HEADLESS=1 cargo test -p winapi` (135 tests), `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2`, and a 20-second headless smoke (`Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 3.1M`, exit 124, no `missing.txt`) all pass. Next blocker: continue the remaining audit (kernel32 console, winmm, dsound/dinput, advapi32) or a new scripted path.
|- `kernel32::misc` console/exception handler pointer hardening: `SetUnhandledExceptionFilter` stored any pointer, and `SetConsoleCtrlHandler` registered any non-null pointer, so a sub-0x1000 exception or console control handler could be recorded and later dispatched into the emulated null page. `SetUnhandledExceptionFilter` now treats pointers below `0x1000` as `0` (no filter), and `SetConsoleCtrlHandler` ignores low-pointer add/remove operations. Added `low_pointer_exception_and_console_handlers_are_ignored` covering both functions. `rustfmt --edition 2024 win32/winapi/src/kernel32/misc.rs`, `cargo clippy -p winapi --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `THESEUS_HEADLESS=1 cargo test -p winapi` (136 tests), `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2`, and a 20-second headless smoke (`Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 3.1M`, exit 124, no `missing.txt`) all pass. Next blocker: continue the remaining audit (winmm, dsound/dinput, advapi32) or a new scripted path.
|- `winmm::mci` null/low pointer hardening: `mciSendCommandA` with the `MCI_STATUS` message read `dwParam2 + 8` and wrote `dwParam2 + 4` without first checking the parameter block pointer, so a sub-0x1000 `dwParam2` would touch the emulated null page. The `MCI_STATUS` branch now returns success without writing when `dwParam2` is below `0x1000`; out-of-range and null pointers continue to be handled by the existing `Ptr` read/write paths. The `mci_status_writes_the_return_field` test now covers `0x500` and `0` parameter pointers. `rustfmt --edition 2024 win32/winapi/src/winmm/mmio.rs`, `cargo clippy -p winapi --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `THESEUS_HEADLESS=1 cargo test -p winapi` (136 tests), `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2`, and a 20-second headless smoke (`Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 3.1M`, exit 124, no `missing.txt`) all pass. Next blocker: continue the remaining audit (winmm wave/time/mixer, dsound/dinput, advapi32) or a new scripted path.
|- `winmm::time` null/low pointer hardening: `timeSetEvent` accepted any `lpTimeProc` value for `TIME_CALLBACK_FUNCTION` once the mode bits were valid, so a sub-0x1000 function pointer could be recorded and later dispatched into the emulated null page by the timer worker. It now rejects `lpTimeProc` below `0x1000` for all callback modes (null/low function pointers and invalid low event handles). The redundant null-callback match arm was removed. Added `time_set_event_rejects_null_and_low_pointers` test. `rustfmt --edition 2024 win32/winapi/src/winmm/time.rs`, `cargo clippy -p winapi --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `THESEUS_HEADLESS=1 cargo test -p winapi` (137 tests), `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2`, and a 20-second headless smoke (`Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 3.1M`, exit 124, no `missing.txt`) all pass.
|- `winmm::wave` callback pointer hardening: `waveOutOpen` with `CALLBACK_FUNCTION` accepted any `dwCallback` value and stored it for the feeder thread; a sub-0x1000 function pointer would eventually call `ctx.indirect` and log a missing address / halt. `waveOutOpen` now rejects `dwCallback` below `0x1000` for any non-null callback mode with `MMSYSERR_INVALPARAM` before the `WAVE_FORMAT_QUERY` fast return, so malformed query and open calls fail cleanly. Also moved the flag/callback validation ahead of the query path so invalid flags and unsupported callback styles are rejected even during format queries. Added `wave_out_open_rejects_low_callback_pointer` test. `rustfmt --edition 2024 win32/winapi/src/winmm/wave.rs`, `cargo clippy -p winapi --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `THESEUS_HEADLESS=1 cargo test -p winapi` (138 tests), `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2`, and a 20-second headless smoke (`Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 3.1M`, exit 124, no `missing.txt`) all pass. Next blocker: remaining audit categories (winmm mixer, dsound/dinput, advapi32) or a new scripted path; no crash or missing-address blocker is reproducible.
|- `advapi32` registry pointer hardening: `RegOpenKeyExA` and `RegCreateKeyExW` took subkey and result pointers and passed them to `ctx.memory.read_str/read_wstr` and `write_out` without first rejecting sub-0x1000 addresses, so a malformed call could read from or fail to write into the emulated null page and still return success. Both now reject `phkResult` below `0x1000` and non-null `lpSubKey` below `0x1000` with `ERROR_INVALID_PARAMETER`; `lpSubKey == 0` is still treated as opening/creating the same key. Added assertions for `0x500` subkey and result pointers. `rustfmt --edition 2024 win32/winapi/src/advapi32.rs`, `cargo clippy -p winapi --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `THESEUS_HEADLESS=1 cargo test -p winapi` (137 tests), `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2`, and a 20-second headless smoke (`Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 3.1M`, exit 124, no `missing.txt`) all pass. Next blocker: remaining audit categories (winmm wave/mixer, dsound/dinput) or a new scripted path; no crash or missing-address blocker is reproducible.
|- `kernel32::file` path pointer hardening: `GetFileAttributesA`, `CreateFileA`, `CreateDirectoryA`, `DeleteFileA`, `SetCurrentDirectoryA`, `GetDriveTypeA`, and `FindFirstFileA` all read path strings from guest memory without first rejecting sub-0x1000 pointers, so a malformed call could read from the emulated null page and act on a bogus path. Added `read_path`, a single helper that rejects addresses below `0x1000` (preserving the documented null-pointer semantics for `GetDriveTypeA`) and converted all seven APIs to use it; `CreateFileA` and `FindFirstFileA` also set `ERROR_INVALID_PARAMETER`. Added `file_path_apis_reject_null_page_pointers` covering the low-pointer and null cases. `rustfmt --edition 2024 win32/winapi/src/kernel32/file.rs`, `cargo clippy -p winapi --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `THESEUS_HEADLESS=1 cargo test -p winapi` (139 tests), `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2`, and a 20-second headless smoke (`Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 3.1M`, exit 124, no `missing.txt`) all pass.
|- `user32::window` window-procedure pointer hardening: `RegisterClassA` resolved `lpfnWndProc` with `ctx.indirect` after only checking for a null pointer, so a sub-0x1000 window procedure would be stored and later dispatched to a missing/halted continuation. It now rejects any window-procedure address below `0x1000` (which also covers the null case) and returns 0. Added `register_class_rejects_null_and_low_wndproc` test. `rustfmt --edition 2024 win32/winapi/src/user32/window.rs`, `cargo clippy -p winapi --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `THESEUS_HEADLESS=1 cargo test -p winapi` (140 tests), `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2`, and a 20-second headless smoke (`Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 3.1M`, exit 124, no `missing.txt`) all pass. Next blocker: remaining audit categories (winmm mixer, dsound/dinput, gdi32) or a new scripted path; no crash or missing-address blocker is reproducible.
|- `shell32::ShellExecuteA` string pointer hardening: `ShellExecuteA` only checked whether `lpFile` was null before calling `read_str`, so a sub-0x1000 file/operation/parameters/directory pointer would read from the emulated null page. It now requires all non-null string pointers to be at least `0x1000`; a null or low `lpFile` returns `SE_ERR_FNF` (2). Added `shell_execute_rejects_null_page_strings` test. `rustfmt --edition 2024 win32/winapi/src/shell32.rs`, `cargo clippy -p winapi --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `THESEUS_HEADLESS=1 cargo test -p winapi` (141 tests), `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2`, and a 20-second headless smoke (`Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 3.1M`, exit 124, no `missing.txt`) all pass. Next blocker: remaining audit categories (winmm mixer, dsound/dinput, gdi32) or a new scripted path; pass budget exhausted after this milestone.
- `user32::CreateWindowEx` low-title iteration: `CreateWindowExA`/`CreateWindowExW` called `read_str`/`read_wstr` on `lpWindowName` without rejecting a low non-null pointer, so a bad guest pointer logged an error and still produced a window. Added `addr != 0 && addr < 0x1000` guards returning a null `HWND` before `create_window` runs, keeping the legal null-title case, plus a `create_window_rejects_low_window_name` regression covering both A and W exports. `rustfmt --edition 2024 --check win32/winapi/src/user32/window.rs`, `cargo clippy -p winapi --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `THESEUS_HEADLESS=1 cargo test -p winapi` (142 tests), `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2`, and a 20-second headless smoke run all pass with no `out/mm2/missing.txt`. Next blocker: the remaining unguarded guest-pointer `read_str` sites in `register_class` (null `lpszClassName` registers a nameless class instead of failing), `advapi32::value_name`, `kernel32::event`/`misc`, `winmm::mmioOpenA`, and `gdi32::CreateFontA`, or a different scripted menu path.
- `user32::register_class` null-class-name iteration: `register_class` mapped `lpszClassName == 0` to `name: None` and still registered the class, but Windows fails a registration with no class name. It now returns 0 for a null `lpszClassName` (after the existing wndproc check), and the name computation no longer has a `None` arm. The `register_class_rejects_null_and_low_wndproc` test now also covers a valid wndproc with a null name. `rustfmt --edition 2024 --check win32/winapi/src/user32/window.rs`, `cargo clippy -p winapi --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `THESEUS_HEADLESS=1 cargo test -p winapi` (142 tests), `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2`, and a 20-second headless smoke (`Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 3.1M`, exit 124, no `missing.txt`) all pass. Next blocker: the remaining unguarded guest-pointer `read_str` sites in `advapi32::value_name` (low non-null silently becomes the default value), `kernel32::event`/`misc`, `winmm::mmioOpenA`, and `gdi32::CreateFontA`, or a different scripted menu path.
- `advapi32` value-name/data pointer hardening: `value_name` treated any non-null `lpValueName` as a string, so a sub-0x1000 pointer resolved through `read_str`'s error path to the default value instead of failing; `RegQueryValueExW`/`RegSetValueExW` had the same inline pattern for wide names. `value_name` now returns `Option` (null still selects the default value, low non-null yields `None` → `ERROR_INVALID_PARAMETER`) and all three exports share it. `reg_query_value` also rejected no low out-pointers — `lpType`, `lpData`, and `lpcbData` below `0x1000` would read or write the emulated null page through `Ptr`/`bytes.get_mut` — so they now return `ERROR_INVALID_PARAMETER`, and `RegSetValueExW` rejects a low non-null `lpData`. The `registry_apis_reject_out_of_range_guest_pointers` test covers low name, type, data, and size pointers. `rustfmt --edition 2024 win32/winapi/src/advapi32.rs`, `cargo clippy -p winapi --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `THESEUS_HEADLESS=1 cargo test -p winapi` (142 tests), `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2`, and a 20-second headless smoke (`Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 3.1M`, exit 124, no `missing.txt`) all pass. Next blocker: the remaining unguarded guest-pointer `read_str` sites in `kernel32::event` (`CreateEventA` low `lpName`), `kernel32::misc` (`OutputDebugStringA`), `winmm::mmioOpenA`, and `gdi32::CreateFontA`, or a different scripted menu path.
- `kernel32::event`/`misc` low-pointer hardening: `CreateEventA` treated any non-null `lpName` as a string, so a sub-0x1000 name produced an unnamed event after a `read_str` error instead of failing; it now returns a null `HANDLE` for a low non-null name (null still creates an unnamed event). `OutputDebugStringA` read any non-null string pointer; it now skips pointers below `0x1000` instead of logging a null-page read. Added `kernel32::event::tests::create_event_rejects_low_name_pointer`. `rustfmt --edition 2024` on both files, `cargo clippy -p winapi --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2`, and a 20-second headless smoke (`Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 3.1M`, exit 124, no `missing.txt`) all pass. `THESEUS_HEADLESS=1 cargo test -p winapi` (143 tests) showed one intermittent `user32::window` `CLASS_LOCK` failure cluster in one of six runs — the pre-existing shared-`State` `RefCell` race noted in earlier entries (a panic in one parallel test poisons the lock for the rest); five consecutive runs are clean and the failure class is unchanged by this work. Next blocker: `winmm::mmioOpenA` and `gdi32::CreateFontA` low-pointer `read_str` sites, or a different scripted menu path.
- `winmm::mmioOpenA` low-filename hardening: the open path rejected only `szFilename == 0`, so a sub-0x1000 name reached `read_str`, logged a null-page error, and then failed anyway on the empty path. It now rejects any `szFilename` below `0x1000` with a null HMMIO up front. `mmio_calls_reject_out_of_range_guest_pointers` covers the null and 0x500 cases. `rustfmt --edition 2024 --check win32/winapi/src/winmm/mmio.rs`, `cargo clippy -p winapi --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `THESEUS_HEADLESS=1 cargo test -p winapi` (143 tests), `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2`, and a 20-second headless smoke (`Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 3.1M`, exit 124, no `missing.txt`) all pass. Next blocker: `gdi32::CreateFontA` low `lpszFace` and a `read_wstr` low-pointer sweep, or a different scripted menu path.
- `gdi32::CreateFontA` low-face-pointer hardening: a sub-0x1000 non-null `lpszFace` reached `read_str` and produced a font with an empty face after a null-page error log; it now returns a null `HFONT` (a null name still maps to the default face). Added `create_font_rejects_low_face_pointer`. `rustfmt --edition 2024 win32/winapi/src/gdi32/object.rs`, `cargo clippy -p winapi --all-targets -- -D warnings`, and `THESEUS_HEADLESS=1 cargo test -p winapi` (144 tests, new test passes; the suite flaked once on the known `user32::window` `CLASS_LOCK`-poison race, root cause identified as `user32::rect`'s test mutating `state().window` without the lock) all pass, along with `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2` and a 20-second headless smoke (`Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 3.1M`, exit 124, no `missing.txt`). Next blocker: serialize `user32::rect`'s window-slot test under the shared lock to kill the recurring `CLASS_LOCK` poison flake, then the `read_wstr` low-pointer sweep.
- Shared-window-state test isolation fix: `user32::rect`'s `client_to_screen_offsets_by_the_window_origin` installed a `Window` into `state().window` without any lock, so its `borrow()`/`borrow_mut()` raced `user32::window`'s `CLASS_LOCK` tests — when the rect test held a borrow during a window test's `borrow_mut()` (or vice versa), the panic under `CLASS_LOCK` poisoned it and all seven window tests failed with `PoisonError`. `CLASS_LOCK` is now the crate-visible `user32::WINDOW_STATE_LOCK` (cfg(test)-only) shared by both modules, and the rect test takes it. `rustfmt --edition 2024` on the three files, `cargo clippy -p winapi --all-targets -- -D warnings`, ten consecutive `THESEUS_HEADLESS=1 cargo test -p winapi` runs (144 tests, zero failures — the flake previously hit roughly every other run), `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2`, and a 20-second headless smoke (`Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 3.1M`, exit 124, no `missing.txt`) all pass. Next blocker: the `read_wstr` low-pointer sweep (`user32::resource`/`UnregisterClassW` path is atom-guarded; remaining candidates are in `kernel32`/`user32` wide-string readers) or a different scripted menu path.
- Wide-string/varargs low-pointer sweep: `kernel32::misc`'s local `read_wstr` (the GetPrivateProfile* path) treated any non-null address as readable, so a sub-0x1000 pointer logged a null-page error before yielding ""; it now returns empty for the whole null page. `user32::misc`'s `MessageBoxW` read closure got the same guard. `wsprintfA`/`wsprintfW` wrote their output through `bytes.get_mut(dst..)`, which bypasses `check_access`, so a destination in the null page was silently scribbled; both now return 0 for `dst < 0x1000`. The shared `wsprintf_impl` `%s` path also guards low string pointers instead of logging a null-page read. The two existing wsprintf tests used sub-0x1000 stack/format/buffer addresses and were moved above the null page; `wsprintf_tolerates_an_out_of_range_destination` now also covers a null-page destination (returns 0) and a low `%s` argument (formats empty). `rustfmt --edition 2024` on both files, `cargo clippy -p winapi --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `THESEUS_HEADLESS=1 cargo test -p winapi` (144 tests), `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2`, and a 20-second headless smoke (`Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 3.1M`, exit 124, no `missing.txt`) all pass. Next blocker: a fresh audit category — e.g. `Ptr::read`/`write` results ignored on required outputs, or `ctx.indirect` dispatch sites with unvalidated guest function pointers — or a different scripted menu path.
- Guest function-pointer dispatch hardening: a dozen `ctx.indirect(ptr)` sites took guest-controlled callback addresses checked only against `== 0` (or not at all), so a sub-0x1000 callback would hit `indirect`'s dump-and-halt path and kill the run instead of failing the API call. All enum-style callbacks now reject pointers below `0x1000` with the same result they already return for null: `DirectDrawEnumerateA`/`ExA` (`DD::ERR_GENERIC`), `ddraw1`/`ddraw7` `EnumDisplayModes`/`EnumAttachedSurfaces` (`DD::ERR_INVALIDPARAMS`), `ddraw1`/`ddraw7` `EnumSurfaces` (skip enumeration like a null callback), `d3d7` `EnumDevices`/`EnumZBufferFormats`/`EnumTextureFormats` (`DD::ERR_INVALIDPARAMS`), `kernel32::CreateThread` (null `HANDLE`), and `msvcrt::_initterm` (low table entries skipped). `user32::SetTimer` rejects a low non-null `lpTimerFunc`, `DispatchMessageW` treats a sub-0x1000 WM_TIMER `lParam` as window-proc delivery rather than a TIMERPROC call, and both `SendMessageW`/`DispatchMessageW` fall back to the class wndproc when `SetWindowLong(GWL_WNDPROC)` stored an uncallable low pointer. `rustfmt --edition 2024` on the eight touched files, `cargo clippy -p winapi --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `THESEUS_HEADLESS=1 cargo test -p winapi` (144 tests), `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2`, and a 20-second headless smoke (`Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 3.1M`, exit 124, no `missing.txt`) all pass. Next blocker: remaining `ctx.indirect` sites are all internally sourced (dmusic return addresses, winmm timer/wave callbacks validated at registration, registered wndprocs); next audit category is `Ptr::read` results ignored on required outputs or a scripted path needing menu coordinates.

Only known external-content warnings appeared. No code changes; the first non-London in-game path is verified stable headlessly. Next blocker: an SF race-mode path (Blitz/Circuit in SF via the RACES screen) or a scripted drive with injected keys in SF.

Warnings stayed within the known external-content categories plus SF `sp_*` breakable-object LOD notices and impact-sound `.22k` lookups (`trashimpact`, `trafficlightimpact`, `mailimpact1`) triggered by the collision. Next blocker: an SF race-mode path (Blitz/Circuit via RACES) or return to a shared-code audit category.
- `dinput::GetDeviceData` null-page guards: the required `pdwInOut` count out-param was only checked against `== 0`, so a low non-null pointer read the null page for the array capacity and then wrote the event count into the null page; a low non-null `rgdod` event array would likewise scribble there (logged but silent). Both now fail with `DIERR_INVALIDPARAM` — `pdwInOut < 0x1000` or `rgdod` in `(0, 0x1000)` — while a true null `rgdod` keeps its documented count/discard behavior. Added `get_device_data_rejects_null_page_pointers`. `rustfmt --edition 2024` on the file, `cargo clippy -p winapi --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `THESEUS_HEADLESS=1 cargo test -p winapi` (145 tests), `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2`, and a 20-second headless smoke (`Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 3.1M`, exit 124, no `missing.txt`) all pass. Next blocker: the guest-pointer audit has converged across winapi/runtime (strings, wide strings, varargs, out-params, enum/thread/timer/subclass callbacks, memory indexing, stack ops, test-shared state); next sessions should focus on scripted paths — an SF race mode via the RACES screen, or a longer multi-mode soak.

The remaining unguarded-looking `read_str`/`read_wstr` sites are all safe: `kernel32/dll` (GetModuleHandleA/LoadLibraryA guard `lpModuleName`/`lpLibFileName < 0x1000`; GetProcAddress treats `< 0x1000` as an ordinal), `kernel32/resource` and `user32/resource` gate on `is_intresource` (`addr >> 16 == 0`), `kernel32/env` guards names/values, `advapi32` guards subkeys and value names, `wsprintf` `%s`/`dst`/`read_bytes0` paths are guarded or use `.get()`. `rustfmt --edition 2024 --check win32/winapi/src/user32/misc.rs`, `cargo clippy -p winapi --all-targets -- -D warnings`, and `THESEUS_HEADLESS=1 cargo test -p winapi` (145 tests) all pass. - Condvar poison-tolerance sweep: an `unwrap` audit of non-test winapi found three condvar waits using plain `.unwrap()` while the adjacent lock used the poison-tolerant `unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner())` pattern established at `kernel32/event.rs:68`. A guest-triggered panic while holding the lock would poison the primitive and turn the next wait into a host panic. Fixed `kernel32/event.rs`'s `WaitForSingleObject` timeout wait, `winmm/mod.rs`'s `TIMER_COND` wait in the timer thread, and `kernel32/thread.rs`'s `EnterCriticalSection` available wait — all now recover the guard instead of propagating `PoisonError`. The other non-test unwraps audited are safe: `d3d7.rs` debug-probe mutex (diagnostic path only), `locked_state.rs` init-time state, `process.rs` init-fatal expects, and `trace.rs`'s `STATE.get().unwrap()` behind the run loop. `rustfmt --edition 2024` on the three files, `cargo clippy -p winapi --all-targets -- -D warnings`, and `THESEUS_HEADLESS=1 cargo test -p winapi` (145 tests) all pass. Next blocker: remaining `.lock().unwrap()` sites on guest-visible mutexes (critical-section map, GDI/draw state) — same poison class — or a scripted CRUISE/Checkpoint variant.
- Shared-state mutex poison sweep: completed the class started in the condvar pass. `LockedState::from`/`from_or_init` are the single choke point for the `Mutex<Option<State>>` behind `kernel32::lock()`, `gdi32::lock()`, `dsound::lock()`, and `dinput`'s state — one panic while holding it (e.g. a guest-driven path or `from`'s own `as_mut().unwrap()` on uninitialized state) poisoned every later access with `PoisonError`. All non-test `.lock().unwrap()` sites on guest-reachable mutexes now use `unwrap_or_else(|e| e.into_inner())`: `locked_state.rs` (both), `kernel32::init_state`, `dsound::init`, `winmm::state()`, `advapi32::registry()`, `kernel32::thread`'s `CRITICAL_SECTIONS` map (critical_section lookup, Delete, Leave) and per-section state locks (Enter, Leave), and `d3d7`'s warn-once `WARNED_RENDER_STATES`/`WARNED_TEXTURE_STAGE_STATES` sets in the render path. Remaining `.lock().unwrap()` sites are all `#[cfg(test)]` (`user32` CLASS_LOCK/WINDOW_STATE_LOCK, `winmm` TIMER_LOCK, `kernel32::ensure_test_state`) — a poisoned test lock still fails the suite, which is desired. `user32`/`ddraw` use `StaticState`+`RefCell` (single-threaded by design). `rustfmt --edition 2024` on the seven files, `cargo clippy -p winapi --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `THESEUS_HEADLESS=1 cargo test -p winapi` (145 tests), `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2`, and a 20-second headless smoke (`Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 3.1M`, exit 124, no `missing.txt`) all pass. Next blocker: scripted CRUISE/Checkpoint variants or a fresh audit category (e.g. `RefCell` reentrant borrows in user32/ddraw state).
- `RefCell` reentrancy audit + QUICK RACE scripted path: audited every `borrow()`/`borrow_mut()` on the `user32` shared-state cells (`window`, `wndclass`, `message_queue`, `input`) — all borrows are scoped and dropped before any `ctx.call32_x86`/`ctx.indirect` guest callback (`DispatchMessageW` lines 561-584, `SendMessageW` 759-786 take wndproc under a scoped borrow then release before dispatch), and `post_message` only appends to the queue without calling guest code, so no reentrant-borrow panic path exists. No defects, no code change. Scripted run: `RUST_LOG=warn THESEUS_HEADLESS=1 THESEUS_MISSING_ADDRS=out/mm2/missing.txt THESEUS_FRAME_DUMP=/tmp/mm2menu/m.ppm THESEUS_FRAME_DUMP_EVERY=200 THESEUS_INJECT_CLICK="515,447;623,297;623,297;515,449" THESEUS_INJECT_CLICK_MS=9000 THESEUS_INJECT_CLICK_GAP=8000 THESEUS_INJECT_HOLD=0x26@45000 timeout 100 ./target/fast/mm2 game` clicked QUICK RACE (515,447) → two VEHICLE-spinner down clicks → GO DRIVE. Frame dumps show the SELECT VEHICLE screen rendering fully — vehicle preview, performance bars, VEHICLE/COLOR/TRANSMISSION dropdowns, and the yellow description text — so this screen's fonts come from a different (present) source than the missing nodeGetBitmap panels. Exit 124, no `missing.txt`, no panic. Next blocker: vehicle/color/transmission spinner changes (dropdown chevrons may need pixel-exact targets), or a scripted CRUISE/Checkpoint variant.

- FIST/FISTP/FISTTP 64-bit cast cleanup: removing the stale `#[allow(clippy::double_parens, clippy::manual_swap, clippy::unnecessary_cast)]` from `out/mm2/src/lib.rs` caused `cargo clippy -p mm2 --all-targets -- -D warnings` to fail because `tc` emitted `ctx.cpu.fpu.to_int(..., 64) as u64` for 64-bit FIST-family stores — `to_int` already returns `u64`, so the cast is unnecessary. Added `CodeGen::fpu_to_int_expr` in `tc/src/codegen/fpu.rs` to omit the `as u{size}` cast for 64-bit destinations while keeping it for 16- and 32-bit stores, and updated the `codegen_handles_fisttp` expectation. Removed the three stale clippy allows from `out/mm2/src/lib.rs` since the regenerated output is clean. `rustfmt --edition 2024 tc/src/codegen/fpu.rs tc/src/codegen/mod.rs out/mm2/src/lib.rs`, `cargo clippy -p tc -p runtime -p winapi --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `cargo test -p tc` (146 tests), `cargo test -p runtime -p winapi` (153 + 145 tests), `cargo clippy -p mm2 --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2`, `out/mm2/translate.sh` (93.8% coverage, 99,384 blocks, zero `not implemented` panics), and the headless London Blitz script (`RUST_LOG=warn THESEUS_HEADLESS=1 THESEUS_MISSING_ADDRS=out/mm2/missing.txt THESEUS_INJECT_AT_MS=5000 THESEUS_INJECT_VKEY=0x0d,0x0d,0x28,0x28,0x28,0x28,0x0d THESEUS_INJECT_CLICK="540,450;530,440" THESEUS_INJECT_CLICK_MS=15000 THESEUS_INJECT_CLICK_GAP=2000 THESEUS_INJECT_HOLD=0x26@45000 timeout 60 ./target/fast/mm2 game`) all pass — the run reached `AIMAP.Init: Done!` and `Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 12.2M`, exited by `timeout` (exit 124), and produced no `out/mm2/missing.txt`. The private decrypted PE remains unavailable; next blocker: continue the shared-code audit or another scripted path.
- `runtime/src/fpu.rs::fxsave` direct-index hardening: `fxsave` used `memory[addr..addr.saturating_add(512)].fill(0)`, which relies on `Memory`'s `IndexMut` fallback to an empty dummy slice when the range is out of bounds — the 512-byte zeroing silently drops and the following `memory.write` calls then log out-of-bounds errors. Replaced the direct-index fill with `memory.write_bytes(addr, &[0u8; 512])`, consistent with `store_env`/`store_env16` and with bounded `Memory::write_bytes` behavior (logs and returns on OOB instead of touching the dummy buffer). Added `fxsave_out_of_bounds_does_not_panic` regression test. `rustfmt --edition 2024 runtime/src/fpu.rs`, `cargo clippy -p runtime --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `cargo test -p runtime` (154 tests), `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2`, and a 20-second headless smoke (`RUST_LOG=warn THESEUS_HEADLESS=1 THESEUS_MISSING_ADDRS=out/mm2/missing.txt timeout 20 ./target/fast/mm2 game`, reached `Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 3.0M`, exit 124, no `out/mm2/missing.txt`) all pass. Next blocker: continue the shared-code audit or another scripted path.
- `kernel32::FileTimeToLocalFileTime` input validation: the function used `lpFileTime.read(...).unwrap_or(0)`, so a bad input pointer produced a zero local file time and returned success; it now returns false when the input FILETIME cannot be read, matching the error handling in `FileTimeToSystemTime`. A regression test covers the copy, zero-identity, and bad-input/output pointer cases. `rustfmt --edition 2024 win32/winapi/src/kernel32/time.rs`, `cargo clippy -p winapi --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `THESEUS_HEADLESS=1 cargo test -p winapi` (146 tests), `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2`, and a 25-second headless smoke (`Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 3.0M`, exit 124, no `missing.txt`) all pass. Next blocker: continue the shared-code audit (`Ptr::read`/`write` results ignored on required inputs/outputs, or the next scripted path).
- `kernel32::InterlockedIncrement`/`InterlockedDecrement` input validation: both functions used `Addend.read(...).unwrap_or_default()` and ignored the `Ptr::write` result, so a bad pointer returned an arbitrary 1 or -1 while silently failing to update memory. They now return 0 when the pointer cannot be read, and a regression test verifies valid round-trips and the bad-pointer path. `rustfmt --edition 2024 win32/winapi/src/kernel32/thread.rs`, `cargo clippy -p winapi --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `THESEUS_HEADLESS=1 cargo test -p winapi` (147 tests), `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2`, and a 25-second headless smoke (`Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 3.0M`, exit 124, no `missing.txt`) all pass. Next blocker: continue the shared-code audit (`Ptr::read`/`write` results ignored on required inputs/outputs, or the next scripted path).
- `kernel32::ReadFile` output-count validation: the `lpNumberOfBytesRead` pointer was written with `let _ = ...write(...)`, so a non-null but out-of-bounds count pointer left the call returning `true` after silently failing to report the byte count. The function now returns `false` when the count cannot be written. A regression test creates a temp file, reads from it, and verifies the bad-pointer path. `rustfmt --edition 2024 win32/winapi/src/kernel32/file.rs`, `cargo clippy -p winapi --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `THESEUS_HEADLESS=1 cargo test -p winapi` (148 tests), `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2`, and a 25-second headless smoke (`Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 3.0M`, exit 124, no `missing.txt`) all pass. Next blocker: continue the shared-code audit (`WriteFile` count pointer, or the next scripted path).
- `kernel32::WriteFile` output-count validation: the console and file branches both wrote `lpNumberOfBytesWritten` with `let _ = ...write(...)`, so a non-null but out-of-bounds count pointer left the call returning success after silently failing to report the byte count. Both branches now return `0` when the count cannot be written. A regression test creates a temp file, writes to it, and verifies the bad-pointer path. `rustfmt --edition 2024 win32/winapi/src/kernel32/file.rs`, `cargo clippy -p winapi --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `THESEUS_HEADLESS=1 cargo test -p winapi` (149 tests), `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2`, and a 25-second headless smoke (`Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 3.0M`, exit 124, no `missing.txt`) all pass. - `GetComputerNameA`/`GetUserNameA` output pointer validation: both functions wrote the terminating null byte and the returned size with unchecked `ctx.memory.write`/`Ptr::write` calls, so a non-null but out-of-range size or terminator pointer returned `true` after silently dropping the write. They now return `false` when either the terminator or the size cannot be written, and the existing tests were extended to cover the bad size-pointer path. `rustfmt --edition 2024 win32/winapi/src/kernel32/misc.rs win32/winapi/src/advapi32.rs`, `cargo clippy -p winapi --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `THESEUS_HEADLESS=1 cargo test -p winapi` (149 tests), `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2`, and a 25-second headless smoke (`Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 3.0M`, exit 124, no `missing.txt`) all pass. - `ddraw7::GetDC` output pointer validation: `GetDC` allocated a surface DC and wrote its handle through `let _ = Ptr::new(lphDC).write(...)`, so a non-null but out-of-range output pointer would leak a DC and return `DD_OK` without writing. - `ddraw7::GetAvailableVidMem` output pointer validation: the method wrote the total and free video-memory `u32` values through `let _ = Ptr::new(...).write(...)`, so a non-null but out-of-range output pointer returned `DD_OK` without writing. - `dinput::GetDeviceData` count pointer validation: with a null event array, the method skipped reading `pdwInOut` and then wrote the count through `let _ = Ptr::new(pdwInOut).write(...)`, so a non-null but out-of-range count pointer when only requesting the event count returned `DI_OK` without writing. It now reads `pdwInOut` up front, so an unreadable count pointer fails with `DIERR_INVALIDPARAM` before any events are processed. `rustfmt --edition 2024 win32/winapi/src/dinput.rs`, `cargo clippy -p winapi --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `THESEUS_HEADLESS=1 cargo test -p winapi` (149 tests), `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2`, and a 25-second headless smoke (`Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 3.0M`, exit 124, no `missing.txt`) all pass. - `dinput::GetDeviceData` per-event destination validation: the event-loop computed each entry address but only checked arithmetic overflow, then wrote each `DIDEVICEOBJECTDATA` with `let _ = ...write(...)`. An entry address that was in-range for overflow but past the end of emulated memory silently dropped that event while still returning `DI_OK`. It now checks `guest_range` before each write and continues past entries that do not fit, making the skipped-event behavior explicit and bounded. `rustfmt --edition 2024 win32/winapi/src/dinput.rs`, `cargo clippy -p winapi --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `THESEUS_HEADLESS=1 cargo test -p winapi` (149 tests), `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2`, and a 25-second headless smoke (`Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 3.0M`, exit 124, no `missing.txt`) all pass. - `dinput::CreateEffect` out-pointer validation: the failure path wrote a null effect pointer with `let _ = ...write(...)` whenever `lplpde` was non-null, which could hit a missing-address log if the pointer was out-of-range. |- `dinput::SetDataFormat` validation + negotiated buffer size: the method returned `DI_OK` unconditionally and never read `lpdf`, so a null or malformed `DIDATAFORMAT` was silently accepted and `GetDeviceState` guessed buffer layouts per device kind (with an arbitrary 256-byte joystick ceiling). `SetDataFormat` now requires a readable 24-byte `DIDATAFORMAT` with `dwSize == 24`, nonzero `dwObjSize`/`dwDataSize`, and — when `dwNumObjs` is nonzero — a readable `rgodf` object table; it stores `dwDataSize` on the device. `GetDeviceState` extends the joystick `cbData` acceptance range to a negotiated size, so a `DIJOYSTATE2` format larger than 256 bytes is honored. Added `set_data_format_validates_the_didataformat` covering null/low/far/wrong-size pointers, the recorded size, and the widened `GetDeviceState` acceptance. `rustfmt --edition 2024 --check win32/winapi/src/dinput.rs`, `cargo clippy -p winapi --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `THESEUS_HEADLESS=1 cargo test -p winapi` (156 tests), `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2`, and a 25-second headless smoke (`Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 3.1M`, exit 124, no `out/mm2/missing.txt`) all pass. Next blocker: continue the shared-code audit or a scripted path (brake/reverse 0x28 input, EVENT RESULTS navigation).
|- `dinput::EnumDevices` real enumeration: the method returned `DI_OK` without ever invoking `lpCallback`, so a caller that discovers devices by enumeration (rather than direct `CreateDevice`) saw an empty system. It now validates the callback against the null page, serializes a `DIDEVICEINSTANCE` for each emulated device (keyboard, mouse, joystick) matching the `dwDevType` filter, allocates the instance on the process heap, and dispatches through `ctx.indirect`/`call32_x86` with `(lpddi, pvRef)`, stopping when the callback returns `DIENUM_STOP` (0). `GetDeviceInfo` was refactored to share the new `write_device_instance` serializer. `rustfmt --edition 2024 --check win32/winapi/src/dinput.rs`, `cargo clippy -p winapi --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `THESEUS_HEADLESS=1 cargo test -p winapi` (156 tests), `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2`, a 20-second `RUST_LOG=debug` run (clean render loop, no dinput errors), and a 25-second headless smoke (`Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 3.1M`, exit 124, no `out/mm2/missing.txt`) all pass. Frame dumps show *progressive* rainbow/static texture corruption: early frames (00:16–00:18) are clean with only small missing-texture spots (distant banners, sign faces), while later frames (00:39+) show the corruption spreading over the car body, road surfaces, and building rooflines. The spread correlates with distance travelled, consistent with missing streamed area/LOD content in the local installation, but a surface-cache defect is not ruled out — worth a future probe using the `d3d7` texture-dump debug path to see whether the corrupt texels come from absent assets or stale guest memory. |- Progressive texture-corruption investigation: `RUST_LOG=debug THESEUS_HEADLESS=1 THESEUS_INJECT_CLICK="515,331;110,102;515,449;530,440" THESEUS_INJECT_CLICK_MS=8000 THESEUS_INJECT_CLICK_GAP=4000 timeout 45 ./target/fast/mm2 game` through city load shows every texture surface is 16bpp — RGB565 (`masks=0xf800,0x7e0,0x1f,0x0`), A1R5G5B5, or mipmapped A4R4G4B4 (`masks=0xf00,0xf0,0xf,0xf000`, `mips=3..8`) — all of which `decode_texel`/`tex_fmt` classification in `win32/winapi/src/ddraw/d3d7.rs` handles, so the rainbow corruption is not a texel-format or stride misdecode. The same run logs zero "never locked or blitted" and zero "unrecognized pixel format" diagnostics but 346 missing-content warnings (`nodeGetBitmap`, missing LODs, `.22k` audio). The rainbow static is therefore stale heap bytes inside surface memory whose source art is absent from the local installation — the same thing real hardware would render when the game fails to fill a texture — i.e. missing streamed content, not an emulation defect. No code changes. Next blocker: EVENT RESULTS navigation after a completed race, or the pause-menu quit path.

- `dmusic` `create` E_POINTER consistency: the three per-module `create` functions (`music_object`, `persist_stream`, `segment`) still wrote the zeroed `ppv` through `let _ = ...write(...)` on their `E_NOINTERFACE` paths, unlike the shared `create` which checks the result and reports `E_POINTER`. All six sites now check the write and return `E_POINTER` on failure, matching the shared helper. `rustfmt --edition 2024 --check win32/winapi/src/dmusic.rs`, `cargo clippy -p winapi --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `THESEUS_HEADLESS=1 cargo test -p winapi` (156 tests), `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2`, and a 25-second headless smoke (`Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 3.1M`, timeout kill, no `out/mm2/missing.txt`) all pass. A grep for `let _ = .*\.write\(` in `win32/winapi/src` again returns zero matches. Next blocker: continue the shared-code audit (`Ptr::read` results ignored on required inputs, or a fresh category) or a scripted path.
- `FPU::bstp` out-of-range fix: FBSTP clamped any finite magnitude to the 18-digit bound and stored `999...9`, but real hardware raises the masked invalid-operation exception and stores the packed-BCD indefinite encoding (all `0xff` bytes) when the value does not fit — the same path this code already used for NaN/±∞. The check is now `val.abs() < 1e18` (the 18-digit bound `999999999999999999` itself rounds to `1e18` as an f64, so the comparison uses `1e18`), and finite over-range values store indefinite. The redundant `clamp`/`floor`/`abs < 1` chain was removed since `round` already produces an integral value. New `fbstp_stores_indefinite_for_non_finite_and_too_large_values` test covers NaN, ±∞, `±2e18`, exact `1e18`, and an in-range encode. `rustfmt --edition 2024 --check runtime/src/fpu.rs`, `cargo clippy -p runtime --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `cargo test -p runtime` (155 tests), `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2`, and a 25-second headless smoke (`Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 3.1M`, exit 124, no `out/mm2/missing.txt`) all pass. Next blocker: continue the `runtime/src/ops`/`fpu` semantic audit or a scripted path.
- Callback-dispatch audit (`ctx.indirect` sites): all DirectDraw/Direct3D/DirectInput enumeration callbacks (`DirectDrawEnumerateA`/`ExA`, `IDirectDraw{1,7}::EnumDisplayModes`/`EnumSurfaces`/`EnumAttachedSurfaces`, `IDirect3D7::EnumDevices`/`EnumZOrder`/`EnumPixelFormats`, `IDirectInput::EnumDevices`), `kernel32::CreateThread`, `winmm` `timeSetEvent`, and `user32` window-proc paths already reject callback addresses below `0x1000`. One gap remained in `winmm::wave`: `waveOutOpen` correctly validates `dwCallback` for `CALLBACK_FUNCTION`/`TASK` but still forwarded the raw value to the feeder thread for `CALLBACK_NULL`, where a nonzero `dwCallback` (legal and ignored on real Windows) would be dispatched as a function pointer and halt the process. New `feeder_callback` helper masks `dwCallback` to 0 for every non-FUNCTION/TASK kind. New `wave_out_null_callback_ignores_the_callback_parameter` test. `rustfmt --edition 2024 --check win32/winapi/src/winmm/wave.rs`, `cargo clippy -p winapi --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `THESEUS_HEADLESS=1 cargo test -p winapi` (157 tests; one `dinput::set_and_get_property_reject_null_pointers` failure observed on the first run, passing in isolation and across three repeat full-suite runs — a pre-existing shared-state scheduling flake in `dinput`'s unserialized tests, not related to this change), `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2`, and a 25-second headless smoke (`Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 3.1M`, no `out/mm2/missing.txt`) all pass. Also confirmed the generated `unhandled_interrupt(0x6)` sites are deliberate `int 6` trap entries in an x87 dispatch table (verified `cd 06` bytes in `out/mm2/data/00401000.raw` at `0x58975d` et al.) — dead stubs needing SEH to model, still non-actionable; `int 1`/`int 3`/`hlt` (0xd) sites are likewise dead privileged/fault paths that terminate correctly. Next blocker: a scripted path or another audit axis (e.g., `msvcrt` function-table entries, `user32` message dispatch payload pointers).
- Root-caused the earlier `dinput::set_and_get_property_reject_null_pointers` flake: it panicked with `reentrant init` at `core/src/cell/once.rs` — `user32::STATE`, `ddraw::STATE`, and `d3d7::D3D_STATE` were `std::cell::OnceCell` behind `unsafe impl Sync`, and `OnceCell::get_or_init` is not thread-safe: two test threads racing the initializer both run `State::default` and the loser's `set` panics. This is a genuine latent runtime bug too — `user32::state()` is called from winmm timer/wave feeder threads, so the same race can panic the game at startup. All three statics now use `std::sync::OnceLock` (identical `get_or_init` API, thread-safe init); the `unsafe impl Sync` wrappers stay because the states still expose non-Sync interior types. `rustfmt --edition 2024 --check` on all three files, `cargo clippy -p winapi --all-targets -- -D warnings`, five consecutive `THESEUS_HEADLESS=1 cargo test -p winapi` runs (157 tests each, zero failures), `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2`, and a 25-second headless smoke (`Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 3.1M`, no `out/mm2/missing.txt`) all pass. Next blocker: further audit axes (e.g., `Ptr::read` results ignored on required inputs, `msvcrt` global-state init) or a scripted path.
- Codegen operand-ordering audit of `xchg`/`cmpxchg`/`cmpxchg8b`/`xadd`/`bt*`: found one real defect in `Xadd` — it emitted `set op1` (the source register write) before `set op0`, so for `xadd [eax], eax` (or any form where the destination's effective address uses the source register) the memory write-back re-evaluated the address with the already-updated register and hit the wrong address, same class of bug `Xchg` already guards against. The fix writes the memory operand first when op0 is memory (reg/reg ordering is unchanged and still correct for `xadd eax, eax`, where the source write must happen first). `Cmpxchg` (accumulator write only in the no-memory-write else branch) and `Cmpxchg8b`/`bt*` (address bound to a local before writes) were verified safe. `xadd` does not appear in the mm2 snapshot; this is a shared-compiler correctness fix. New `codegen_xadd_writes_memory_before_updating_the_source_register` test asserts the emitted ordering. `rustfmt --edition 2024 --check tc/src/codegen/{misc,mod}.rs`, `cargo clippy -p tc --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `cargo test -p tc` (147 tests), `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2`, and a 25-second headless smoke (`Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 3.1M`, no `out/mm2/missing.txt`) all pass. Next blocker: further audit axes or a scripted path.
- `Mapping::round_to_page` overflow: `(size + 0xfff) & !0xfff` panics in dev builds and wraps to a small reservation in release for sizes ≥ `0xffff_f001` (e.g. a guest `VirtualAlloc` near the top of the address space), after which `try_alloc` would record a bogus small mapping. It now saturates, so `try_alloc` correctly returns `None` past the limit. Verified `pop`/`push`/`call [mem]` ordering is already correct (`pop [esp]` rightly evaluates the destination with post-increment ESP, matching Intel), `enter`/`leave` display-copy semantics match the spec, and `div`/`idiv`/`mul`/`imul` flag and trap paths are sound (`checked_div` + quotient-range trap for idiv, `dividend/divisor > MAX` trap for div, `#DE` dispatched through the DOS IVT in real mode). New `round_to_page_saturates_past_u32_max` and `try_alloc_rejects_sizes_past_the_address_space` tests. `rustfmt --edition 2024 --check runtime/src/mapping.rs`, `cargo clippy -p runtime --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `cargo test -p runtime` (157 tests), `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2`, and a 25-second headless smoke (`Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 3.1M`, no `out/mm2/missing.txt`) all pass. Next blocker: further audit axes or a scripted path.
- `rol`/`ror` masked-count CF fix: both helpers early-returned when `count % width == 0`, but per the Intel SDM the CF update is keyed on the *masked* count (`count & 0x1f`) — `rol al, 8` still writes `CF := LSB(AL)` and `ror al, 8` writes `CF := MSB(AL)` even though the value is unchanged. Both now early-return only when the masked count is zero, and OF writes are gated on `masked == 1` since OF is undefined for larger rotates (previously it was unconditionally overwritten). `rcr`'s OF formula (`MSB(result) ^ MSB-1(result)`) was verified against the spec's pre-rotate `MSB(DEST) XOR CF` — equivalent for 1-bit rotates — and `rcl`/`rcr` correctly leave all flags alone when `masked % (width+1) == 0` since CF is part of the rotation ring. New `full_width_rotates_still_update_cf` test; the `ror` multi-bit case now asserts OF is preserved rather than overwritten. `rustfmt --edition 2024 --check runtime/src/ops/shift.rs`, `cargo clippy -p runtime --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `cargo test -p runtime` (158 tests), `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2`, and a 25-second headless smoke (`Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 3.1M`, no `out/mm2/missing.txt`) all pass. Next blocker: continue audit axes (string/shift/int ops verified clean; remaining: guest-arithmetic overflow in winapi, `host/` crate paths) or a scripted path.

- `FPU::extract` (FXTRACT) exponent rounding fix: `val.abs().log2().floor()` rounds up for inputs just below a power of two — e.g. the largest subnormal `2.225073858507201e-308` produced exponent -1022 and significand `0.9999999999999998`, below the required [1,2) range — and `2f64.powi` underflows to 0 for subnormal exponents, making the significand inf. The exponent now comes straight from the IEEE-754 fields (`biased - 1023` for normals, `ilog2(frac) - 1074` for subnormals) and the `2^exp` divisor is constructed exactly from bits (`(exp+1023) << 52` for exp >= -1022, else `1 << (exp + 1074)`), so the significand always lands in [1,2). New `fxtract_keeps_the_significand_in_range_near_powers_of_two` test covers the largest and smallest subnormals plus the just-below-2.0 case. `rustfmt --edition 2024 --check runtime/src/fpu.rs`, `cargo clippy -p runtime --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `cargo test -p runtime` (159 tests), `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2`, and a 25-second headless smoke (`Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 3.1M`, no `out/mm2/missing.txt`) all pass. Also verified clean this pass: `ops/math.rs` adc/sbb OF/AF/CF (raw-operand formula is correct — the carry-in must not fold into the operand's sign), inc/dec CF preservation, mul/imul overflow flags, string ops and REP prefix semantics in `ops/string.rs` + `tc/src/codegen/string.rs`, `ops/control_flow.rs` ret/iret/loop edge cases, pushf/popf reserved-bit handling, winapi guest-range arithmetic (`checked_add`/`guest_range`/`nCount` caps all sound), `heap.rs` allocator overflow paths, msvcrt low-pointer handling, and clippy across runtime/tc/winapi/host/exe/mm2 (zero warnings). Next blocker: remaining audit surface is thin — `xmm.rs` ops, `tc` decoder prefix edge cases, or another scripted path (Circuit/Checkpoint/Cruise race modes).
- `Regs::get_dx_ax` high-word leak fix: the helper returned `(edx << 16) | eax`, OR-ing the full 32-bit EAX into the low half of the DX:AX dividend, so 16-bit `div`/`idiv` read garbage whenever the guest had data in EAX's high word (routine in 32-bit code) — producing wrong quotients or spurious #DE. It now masks both halves (`(edx & 0xffff) << 16 | (eax & 0xffff)`). Callers verified: `div`/`idiv` 16-bit dividend reads and `cwd`'s `set_dx_ax` in `tc/src/codegen/math.rs`/`misc.rs`; `get_dx_ax` does not appear in the current mm2 snapshot, so this is a shared-runtime correctness fix. Also verified clean: `runtime/src/machine.rs` dispatch (BlockCache keyed lookup + binary-search fallback + missing-addr logging), `runtime/src/lib.rs` (cpuid FPU|TSC|CX8|MMX, call32_x86 stack protocol), `ops/xmm.rs` conversion indefinite-encoding range checks and minps/maxps src2-on-tie semantics, `kernel32/heap.rs` API layer, `kernel32` VirtualAlloc/VirtualFree. New `dx_ax_ignores_the_high_words_of_edx_and_eax` test. `rustfmt --edition 2024 --check runtime/src/registers.rs`, `cargo clippy -p runtime --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `cargo test -p runtime` (160 tests), `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2`, and a 25-second headless smoke (`Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 3.1M`, no `out/mm2/missing.txt`) all pass. Next blocker: audit surface continues to converge — remaining axes are `tc` decoder/codegen edge cases (operand/address-size prefix in flat mode, unusual ModRM forms) or more scripted paths.
- `tc/src/gather` audit + off-by-one fix: `scan_for_pointers` iterated `0..data.len().saturating_sub(4)`, which skips the last valid 4-byte window (`ofs = len - 4`), so a code pointer in a mapping's final dword was never scanned. Now `data.windows(4)` covers every window. Regenerated the snapshot: `out/mm2/translate.sh` completed cleanly (code coverage 0x193313/0x1ae000 = 93.8%, 99,384 blocks — byte-identical generated output, so no pointers hide in those trailing dwords for this image). - `BT`/`BTS`/`BTR`/`BTC` memory-form register-index sign fix: `tc/src/codegen/misc.rs` generated `addr.wrapping_add((bit >> shift).wrapping_mul(bytes))` — a logical shift on the u32 bit index, so a *negative* register index (architecturally signed: `EffectiveAddr = BitBase + (size/8) * (BitOffset SAR n)`) addressed a huge positive offset instead of reaching into the bit string below the base. The element offset now sign-extends the index (`(reg as i32)` for 32-bit, `(reg as i16 as i32)` for 16-bit) and shifts arithmetically, keeping `wrapping_mul`/`wrapping_add` so overflow-checked builds still cannot panic (the motivation for the earlier unsigned version, 1c11daa5 — this restores the signed semantics while preserving its overflow safety). The in-element index remains the unmasked `bit` (u32 low bits are identical under sign extension). Regression test renamed to `codegen_uses_signed_wrapped_offsets_for_memory_bit_indices` and corrected — `bt [eax], ecx` uses ecx, not eax, as the index. Also verified clean: `runtime/src/memory.rs` (null-page checks, checked_add bounds, dummy sinks for OOB indexing), `runtime/src/mapping.rs` (`try_alloc` gap math in u64, saturating `round_to_page`, `insert_index` overlap checks), `runtime/src/ops/bit.rs` (bt* masking, arpl RPL semantics), shld/shrd count masking (`count % 16`/`% 32` is a valid choice for the >operand-size undefined case). `rustfmt --edition 2024`, `cargo clippy -p tc --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `cargo test -p tc` (147 tests), `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2`, and a 25-second headless smoke (`Heap MemUsed: 3.1M`, no `out/mm2/missing.txt`) all pass. - `dos` EXEC panic removal: `dos/src/dosapi.rs` `int 21h AH=4Bh` subfunctions 0 (load+run) and 1 (load-only) used `todo!`, so any DOS guest that execs a child program panicked the host mid-run. The arm now logs a warning and fails the call with CF set and `AX=5` (access denied) — the closest honest code for a refused valid subfunction — instead of aborting. The pointer-sweep and `Ptr::read`-result audits this session found no remaining unguarded sites: every `read_str`/`read_wstr` guest-pointer use is null-page-guarded, and all non-`let-else` `Ptr::read` sites either propagate `Option` or are test/init paths. Remaining `todo!`/`unreachable!`/`panic!` sites are tc translation-time (contained by `gen_block`'s `catch_unwind`), init-fatal expects, or wasm-host-only. `cargo fmt -p dos`, `cargo clippy -p dos --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `cargo test -p dos` (0 tests), and `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2` all pass. Next blocker: tc decoder/codegen prefix edge cases, or multiplayer HOST/JOIN scripted clicks.
- `dos` PIT panic removal: `dos/src/timer.rs` had three guest-reachable panic paths. `PIT::out` asserted `port == 0x40` for the 0x40..=0x42 data range, so a guest programming channel 2 (the PC speaker, ubiquitous in DOS games) or channel 1 panicked the host; the 0x43 control word `assert_eq!`s panicked on any non-square-wave mode, latch/read-back command, BCD mode, or non-zero channel. Writes to channels 1/2 are now accepted and dropped (no speaker/refresh model), and channel-0 control words honor lobyte-only/hibyte-only access modes via a stored `access_mode`, warn on latch/BCD configurations, and reset the lo/hi staging. `call_timer` asserted `seg != 0` and `cs == seg` — a null IVT[8] handler or a handler in a different segment than the interrupted code panicked; a null handler now drops the tick and the pushed frame reports the interrupted CS (the true resume IP is not tracked at this call site). `check_timer` also replayed an unbounded tick backlog after a host stall — it now fires at most 8 queued ticks per check and resyncs. `cargo fmt -p dos`, `cargo clippy -p dos --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `cargo test -p dos -p tc` (147 tc tests), `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2`, and a 20-second headless smoke (`Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 3.1M`, no `out/mm2/missing.txt`) all pass. Next blocker: multiplayer HOST/JOIN scripted clicks or another audit axis.
- `winapi` DirectPlay object: scripted HOST/JOIN clicks in MULTIPLAYER SESSIONS crashed with "jmp to null ptr" — `NETMGR.Initialize()` calls `CoCreateInstance(CLSID_DirectPlay, IID_IDirectPlay4A)`, which was unregistered, so the manager's `[esi+18h]` interface pointer stayed null and the click path did `mov edx,[eax]`/`call [edx+34h]` (`EnumSessions`) on a null vtable. `win32/winapi/src/dplayx.rs` now has a `directplay` module with the full 53-entry `IDirectPlay4A` vtable (order verified against Wine's `dplay.h`: IUnknown + 29 IDirectPlay2 + 15 IDirectPlay3 + 6 IDirectPlay4 slots, correct stdcall arities): `EnumSessions`/`EnumConnections`/`Close`/`Initialize(NULL)` return DP_OK, `Initialize(non-null)` returns DPERR_NOSERVICEPROVIDER, `InitializeConnection` returns DPERR_UNAVAILABLE, and the remaining methods return DPERR_NOCONNECTION — the honest behavior of a DirectPlay install with no service providers. `QueryInterface` answers IUnknown plus IID_IDirectPlay2/2A/3/3A/4/4A (shared vtable prefix; v1's different layout is refused). `ole32::CoCreateInstance` registers `CLSID_DirectPlay`. Debug run confirms: `EnumConnections` + `Release` during NETMGR init (no more "Initialize() failed"), then `EnumSessions` at the previously-crashing call site returns DP_OK and the game logs "StopSessionsAsynch done."; the scripted path MULTIPLAYER→radio→NET NAME→HOST→JOIN→BACK exits cleanly with no `missing.txt`. `cargo fmt -p winapi`, `cargo clippy -p winapi --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `cargo test -p winapi` (157 tests), and `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2` all pass. Next blocker: real network play would need service-provider/session implementation (out of scope); remaining audit axes or longer soaks.
